# MatchIQ requirements traceability matrix

**Generated:** 2026-10-24 | **Audited commit:** `origin/dev` ed46466 (includes PR #80) | **Specification:** `docs/product/MatchIQ_Enterprise_Master_Specification_v3.md` (58 sections; the `.docx` is content-equivalent) | **Companion files:** `MATCHIQ_REQUIREMENTS_TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.csv` (full text, every field) and `MATCHIQ_ENTERPRISE_IMPLEMENTATION_AUDIT.md`.

This is the compact view. The CSV carries every field in full (all evidence references, all test references, blockers, notes). Evidence is `path:lines` on `origin/dev`.

## How to read it

- **Status** is one of: VERIFIED (implemented and validated by a passing automated test or recorded runtime observation), UNVERIFIED (code exists, no adequate test), PARTIAL, MISSING (not implemented), BLOCKED, DEFERRED, N/A.
- **Verification** `CI-passed` means a test in this repository asserts the behaviour and runs in CI, and CI on `dev` is green (runs 40101072960 and 40105654686). `runtime-observed` means observed on the running stack on 2026-10-24. `none` means neither.
- **Scope / priority:** `H` = hackathon, `E` = enterprise (post-hackathon); P0 blocks core function, demo, submission or critical safety; P1 important to quality; P2 post-hackathon enhancement. 840 of 976 priorities are stated by the spec; 136 are inferred (flagged `*`).
- Cross-section restatements of the same requirement are listed under each section and counted separately.
